# 🛣️ P3: Platform features

**Status:** 🟡 Draft · **Sprints:** S16 to S23 · **Dates:** 19 Jul to 5 Nov 2027 · **Tickets:** 95

> [!NOTE]
> P3 builds the platform features every business area relies on, on top of the platform core of P2: administration (F1), no-code configuration (F2), the AI assistant (F3), meetings (F4), onboarding and module activation (F21), unified cost management (F22) and the integrations hub (F24, Proposed placement), so that from P4 on every business capability is configured, guided, metered and reachable from the AI assistant from its first ticket.

[⬆️ Phases](README.md)

## <a id="goal"></a>🎯 Goal

At the end of P2 the platform core exists (companies and isolation, identity, audit, plugins, API, MCP and A2A, AI providers, RAG, install, translations, legal corpora, connectors, maps), but nobody can yet set a company up, configure it, ask the AI assistant or follow what it costs. At the end of P3:

- [F1 Administration](../../../02-functional/f01-administration/README.md): an administrator sets a company up in seven steps, manages its sites, users, roles, record rules, single sign-on, provisioning, security policies, audit and retention, and an IT provider runs its client companies.
- [F2 No-code configuration](../../../02-functional/f02-no-code-configuration/README.md): a configurator adds fields, statuses, forms, record types, templates, rules, workflows, views, bookings, portals and offline forms, previews them, tests them, publishes them and rolls them back.
- [F3 Assistant](../../../02-functional/f03-assistant/README.md): every screen has the AI assistant in its side panel: it answers from what the user may see, with sources and exact figures, acts after confirmation with an Undo, drafts, guides, uses outside tools and runs governed agents.
- [F4 Meetings](../../../02-functional/f04-meetings/README.md): meetings online, in person and by phone become checked transcripts, summaries, decisions and tasks linked to records, with recordings deleted once checked.
- [F21 Onboarding and module activation](../../../02-functional/f21-onboarding-and-modules/README.md): a non-technical owner goes from a brand-new company to its go-live through the first-run guide, switches modules on in one click and migrates from a named tool.
- [F22 Unified platform cost management](../../../02-functional/f22-unified-cost-management/README.md): every paid call is metered, budgeted with warnings and a stop that never blocks a legal duty, estimated, allocated, matched with suppliers' invoices and, in the hosted edition, billed.
- [F24 Integrations hub](../../../02-functional/f24-integrations-hub/README.md): outside tools connect in the fewest steps, the office suites first, with two-way CRM and ATS sync and every action usable from the AI assistant (Proposed placement).

## <a id="sprints"></a>🗓️ Sprint plan

Eight two-week sprints, 11 or 12 tickets each, as many as can run in parallel. Each capability starts in S16 with its service and its first flow, so that the seven capabilities move side by side; the cross-capability tickets (the AI assistant in the guide, costs asked to the AI assistant, rooms through the booking engine) wait for both sides.

| Sprint | Dates | Tickets | Sprint goal | What can be shown |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| [S16](#s16) | 19 to 30 Jul 2027 | T3.01 Setup wizard of a new company · T3.02 User lifecycle · T3.03 Role editor and record rules · T3.04 AI provider per feature · T3.05 Single sign-on set-up · T3.06 Record types and custom fields engine · T3.07 Assistant answer engine · T3.08 Meeting sources and calendars · T3.09 Module catalogue switched in one click · T3.10 Paid providers, prices and keys · T3.11 Connector catalogue and connection | Each capability has its service and its first flow | A new company set up in the wizard; a Vehicles record type with a custom field; a first answer with sources; a calendar connected; a module switched on; a provider's prices; the connector catalogue |

## <a id="demo"></a>🎤 Demo

Given on 22 Oct 2027, the last day of S23, on the laptop profile, then repeated on Kubernetes, with a sample company and a sample IT provider.

| Cast | Plays |
| --- | --- |
| A small French company of 12 people | The company set up during the demonstration |
| Its owner | [U13 Owner](../../../01-users/personas/u13-owner.md) |
| Its administrator | [U07 Administrator](../../../01-users/personas/u07-administrator.md) |
| Its external accountant | [U08 External accountant](../../../01-users/personas/u08-external-accountant.md) |
| A salesperson and a field worker | [U03 Sales](../../../01-users/personas/u03-sales.md), [U14 Field worker](../../../01-users/personas/u14-field-worker.md) |
| The IT provider's administrator | [U11 Provider admin](../../../01-users/personas/u11-provider-admin.md) |

1. **Set up.** The owner starts the first-run guide, describes the company, picks a sector template, types the SIREN and sees the identity prefilled, checks VAT and bank, passes the legal check and asks the AI assistant what a VAT regime is ([UC-F21-01](../../../02-functional/f21-onboarding-and-modules/use-cases/01-first-run/uc-f21-01-start-the-first-run-guide.md), [UC-F21-03](../../../02-functional/f21-onboarding-and-modules/use-cases/01-first-run/uc-f21-03-pick-a-sector-template.md), [UC-F21-12](../../../02-functional/f21-onboarding-and-modules/use-cases/01-first-run/uc-f21-12-ask-the-assistant-at-any-step.md), [UC-F1-01](../../../02-functional/f01-administration/use-cases/01-guided-setup/uc-f01-01-set-up-a-new-company.md)).
2. **Switch modules on.** The owner switches on a module of the catalogue; its dependencies are explained; the hosted price shows before the click; switching a module off keeps its data read-only ([UC-F21-04](../../../02-functional/f21-onboarding-and-modules/use-cases/02-modules/uc-f21-04-switch-modules-on-in-one-click.md), [UC-F21-05](../../../02-functional/f21-onboarding-and-modules/use-cases/02-modules/uc-f21-05-understand-a-module-s-dependencies.md), [UC-F21-06](../../../02-functional/f21-onboarding-and-modules/use-cases/02-modules/uc-f21-06-switch-a-module-off-and-keep-its-data.md)).
3. **Bring people and data in.** The administrator turns single sign-on on for the company's domain, invites the accountant, and imports the partners of the previous tool with its ready mapping ([UC-F1-09](../../../02-functional/f01-administration/use-cases/07-set-up-single-sign-on/uc-f01-09-set-up-single-sign-on.md), [UC-F21-23](../../../02-functional/f21-onboarding-and-modules/use-cases/03-team-and-data/uc-f21-23-invite-the-external-accountant.md), [UC-F21-21](../../../02-functional/f21-onboarding-and-modules/use-cases/05-migration-from-another-tool/uc-f21-21-migrate-from-a-named-tool.md)).
4. **Configure.** A configurator adds a Vehicles record type with a custom field, a kanban view and a booking rule, previews the draft with real data, sees its impact, runs its test cases and publishes it, then rolls it back and forward again ([UC-F2-04](../../../02-functional/f02-no-code-configuration/use-cases/01-configure-a-record-type/uc-f02-04-create-a-new-record-type.md), [UC-F2-17](../../../02-functional/f02-no-code-configuration/use-cases/08-views-and-bookings/uc-f02-17-add-a-kanban-calendar-map-gantt-or-timeline-view.md), [UC-F2-18](../../../02-functional/f02-no-code-configuration/use-cases/08-views-and-bookings/uc-f02-18-make-a-record-type-bookable.md), [UC-F2-09](../../../02-functional/f02-no-code-configuration/use-cases/05-publish-and-roll-back/uc-f02-09-draft-preview-and-publish-a-change.md), [UC-F2-23](../../../02-functional/f02-no-code-configuration/use-cases/10-change-safely/uc-f02-23-see-the-impact-of-a-change-before-publishing.md), [UC-F2-24](../../../02-functional/f02-no-code-configuration/use-cases/10-change-safely/uc-f02-24-test-rules-and-workflows-with-test-cases.md)).
5. **Ask and act.** The salesperson asks the AI assistant how many Vehicles are due for service this month: the answer cites its sources and the figure opens its query; the AI assistant changes the status of three Vehicles after confirmation, and the salesperson undoes the batch ([UC-F3-01](../../../02-functional/f03-assistant/use-cases/01-ask-the-assistant/uc-f03-01-ask-a-question-about-the-company-s-data.md), [UC-F3-16](../../../02-functional/f03-assistant/use-cases/11-trust-the-answers/uc-f03-16-see-the-query-behind-a-figure.md), [UC-F3-03](../../../02-functional/f03-assistant/use-cases/03-act-through-the-assistant/uc-f03-03-have-the-assistant-create-or-change-records.md)).
6. **Meet.** The bot joins a calendar meeting with the customer and announces itself; the organiser reviews the transcript, names the speakers, turns the actions into tasks and sends the follow-up; the recording is deleted once checked ([UC-F4-01](../../../02-functional/f04-meetings/use-cases/01-record-an-online-meeting/uc-f04-01-record-an-online-meeting-with-the-bot.md), [UC-F4-05](../../../02-functional/f04-meetings/use-cases/03-review-a-meeting/uc-f04-05-review-a-meeting.md)).
7. **Follow the costs.** The owner sees all platform costs of the month per module and provider, receives the 80 % warning of the AI budget, and sees new paid calls stop at 100 % while an e-invoicing duty still goes through ([UC-F22-01](../../../02-functional/f22-unified-cost-management/use-cases/01-see-and-set-up/uc-f22-01-see-all-platform-costs-in-one-place.md), [UC-F22-07](../../../02-functional/f22-unified-cost-management/use-cases/02-budgets-and-stops/uc-f22-07-receive-warnings-at-thresholds.md), [UC-F22-08](../../../02-functional/f22-unified-cost-management/use-cases/02-budgets-and-stops/uc-f22-08-stop-paid-calls-at-100-legal-duties-kept.md)).
8. **Connect.** The administrator connects openDesk in the fewest steps, then a CRM of the default 50, maps its fields, and the AI assistant updates a deal in the CRM after confirmation ([UC-F24-02](../../../02-functional/f24-integrations-hub/use-cases/01-find-and-connect/uc-f24-02-connect-a-tool-in-the-fewest-steps.md), [UC-F24-03](../../../02-functional/f24-integrations-hub/use-cases/02-office-suites/uc-f24-03-sign-in-once-through-the-suite-s-identity-provider.md), [UC-F24-10](../../../02-functional/f24-integrations-hub/use-cases/03-crm-and-ats/uc-f24-10-sync-a-crm-both-ways.md), [UC-F24-14](../../../02-functional/f24-integrations-hub/use-cases/04-run-and-control/uc-f24-14-ask-the-assistant-to-act-in-a-connected-tool.md)).
9. **Support a client.** The IT provider's administrator creates a client company from a template, enters it, and the client sees the session in its provider access log ([UC-F1-10](../../../02-functional/f01-administration/use-cases/08-provider-and-client-companies/uc-f01-10-create-a-client-company-on-the-platform.md), [UC-F1-11](../../../02-functional/f01-administration/use-cases/08-provider-and-client-companies/uc-f01-11-enter-a-client-company-to-support-it.md)).

## <a id="exit"></a>🚪 Exit criteria

- [ ] One company runs on the laptop profile (8 GB) and on Kubernetes with every P3 capability switched on (the exit criterion of the platform step of the roadmap).
- [ ] F1 Administration: every use case [UC-F1-01](../../../02-functional/f01-administration/use-cases/01-guided-setup/uc-f01-01-set-up-a-new-company.md) to [UC-F1-27](../../../02-functional/f01-administration/use-cases/15-security-and-group-data/uc-f01-27-act-as-a-user-with-consent.md) has its passing Playwright or pytest test, and every business rule [BR-F1-01](../../../02-functional/f01-administration/business-rules/br-f01-01-offboarding-hand-over.md) to [BR-F1-36](../../../02-functional/f01-administration/business-rules/br-f01-36-acting-as-a-user-needs-consent-and-is-audited.md) its rule test, each linked to its evidence.
- [ ] F2 No-code configuration: every use case [UC-F2-01](../../../02-functional/f02-no-code-configuration/use-cases/01-configure-a-record-type/uc-f02-01-add-a-custom-field-to-a-record-type.md) to [UC-F2-35](../../../02-functional/f02-no-code-configuration/use-cases/13-low-code-bulk-and-cleaning/uc-f02-35-clean-duplicates-and-stale-records.md) has its passing Playwright or pytest test, and every business rule [BR-F2-01](../../../02-functional/f02-no-code-configuration/business-rules/br-f02-01-archive-never-delete.md) to [BR-F2-32](../../../02-functional/f02-no-code-configuration/business-rules/br-f02-32-merges-keep-every-link-and-an-undo-window.md) its rule test, each linked to its evidence.
- [ ] F3 Assistant: every use case [UC-F3-01](../../../02-functional/f03-assistant/use-cases/01-ask-the-assistant/uc-f03-01-ask-a-question-about-the-company-s-data.md) to [UC-F3-24](../../../02-functional/f03-assistant/use-cases/13-voice-agents-and-briefings/uc-f03-24-keep-personal-to-dos-and-memos.md) has its passing Playwright or pytest test, and every business rule [BR-F3-01](../../../02-functional/f03-assistant/business-rules/br-f03-01-answers-cite-their-sources.md) to [BR-F3-47](../../../02-functional/f03-assistant/business-rules/br-f03-47-to-dos-are-private-unless-shared.md) its rule test, each linked to its evidence.
- [ ] F4 Meetings: every use case [UC-F4-01](../../../02-functional/f04-meetings/use-cases/01-record-an-online-meeting/uc-f04-01-record-an-online-meeting-with-the-bot.md) to [UC-F4-31](../../../02-functional/f04-meetings/use-cases/14-video-messages-and-webinars/uc-f04-31-analyse-time-spent-in-meetings.md) has its passing Playwright or pytest test, and every business rule [BR-F4-01](../../../02-functional/f04-meetings/business-rules/br-f04-01-every-recording-is-announced.md) to [BR-F4-36](../../../02-functional/f04-meetings/business-rules/br-f04-36-meeting-analytics-never-show-groups-under-five-people.md) its rule test, each linked to its evidence.
- [ ] F21 Onboarding and module activation: every use case [UC-F21-01](../../../02-functional/f21-onboarding-and-modules/use-cases/01-first-run/uc-f21-01-start-the-first-run-guide.md) to [UC-F21-29](../../../02-functional/f21-onboarding-and-modules/use-cases/06-experts-cutover-and-parallel-run/uc-f21-29-set-up-from-last-years-documents.md) has its passing Playwright or pytest test, and every business rule [BR-F21-01](../../../02-functional/f21-onboarding-and-modules/business-rules/br-f21-01-legal-set-up-from-the-country-pack.md) to [BR-F21-20](../../../02-functional/f21-onboarding-and-modules/business-rules/br-f21-20-settings-proposed-from-documents-are-confirmed-by-a-person.md) its rule test, each linked to its evidence.
- [ ] F22 Unified platform cost management: every use case [UC-F22-01](../../../02-functional/f22-unified-cost-management/use-cases/01-see-and-set-up/uc-f22-01-see-all-platform-costs-in-one-place.md) to [UC-F22-30](../../../02-functional/f22-unified-cost-management/use-cases/08-credits-saas-and-carbon/uc-f22-30-route-ai-tasks-to-save-costs.md) has its passing Playwright or pytest test, and every business rule [BR-F22-01](../../../02-functional/f22-unified-cost-management/business-rules/br-f22-01-costs-follow-the-country-pack.md) to [BR-F22-21](../../../02-functional/f22-unified-cost-management/business-rules/br-f22-21-routing-never-goes-below-a-tasks-quality-bar.md) its rule test, each linked to its evidence.
- [ ] F24 Integrations hub (Proposed placement): every use case [UC-F24-01](../../../02-functional/f24-integrations-hub/use-cases/01-find-and-connect/uc-f24-01-browse-the-connector-catalogue.md) to [UC-F24-31](../../../02-functional/f24-integrations-hub/use-cases/08-ai-connectors-suites-and-warehouse/uc-f24-31-sync-with-the-accountants-software.md) has its passing Playwright or pytest test, and every business rule [BR-F24-01](../../../02-functional/f24-integrations-hub/business-rules/br-f24-01-connectors-follow-the-country-pack.md) to [BR-F24-20](../../../02-functional/f24-integrations-hub/business-rules/br-f24-20-one-side-is-the-master-per-period.md) its rule test, each linked to its evidence.
- [ ] The AI assistant never shows a record the user may not see, through answers, explanations, shared conversations, briefings and add-ins ([BR-F3-02](../../../02-functional/f03-assistant/business-rules/br-f03-02-answers-use-only-what-the-user-may-see.md), [BR-F3-06](../../../02-functional/f03-assistant/business-rules/br-f03-06-explanations-never-reveal-hidden-data.md), [BR-F3-46](../../../02-functional/f03-assistant/business-rules/br-f03-46-briefings-show-only-what-the-reader-may-see.md), [BR-F3-45](../../../02-functional/f03-assistant/business-rules/br-f03-45-add-ins-use-the-users-own-rights.md)), proven by the permission suite on every pull request.
- [ ] The AI assistant never deletes and every change it makes is confirmed, signed and undoable ([BR-F3-07](../../../02-functional/f03-assistant/business-rules/br-f03-07-no-deletes-no-irreversible-acts.md), [BR-F3-08](../../../02-functional/f03-assistant/business-rules/br-f03-08-a-batch-is-undone-as-a-whole.md), [BR-F3-10](../../../02-functional/f03-assistant/business-rules/br-f03-10-changes-signed-through-the-assistant.md)).
- [ ] The quality evaluation set passes on the default local model and blocks a change that lowers it ([UC-F3-14](../../../02-functional/f03-assistant/use-cases/11-trust-the-answers/uc-f03-14-run-the-quality-evaluation-set.md), [BR-F3-37](../../../02-functional/f03-assistant/business-rules/br-f03-37-a-quality-drop-blocks-a-model-or-prompt-change.md)).
- [ ] Every paid call of P3 goes through the metering client and the stop at 100 % never blocks a legal duty ([BR-F22-02](../../../02-functional/f22-unified-cost-management/business-rules/br-f22-02-every-paid-call-metered.md), [BR-F22-06](../../../02-functional/f22-unified-cost-management/business-rules/br-f22-06-stop-at-budget-legal-duties-kept.md)).
- [ ] Every recording is announced and deleted once checked ([BR-F4-01](../../../02-functional/f04-meetings/business-rules/br-f04-01-every-recording-is-announced.md), [BR-F4-03](../../../02-functional/f04-meetings/business-rules/br-f04-03-recordings-are-deleted-once-checked.md)), against [meeting recording](../../../05-non-functional/meeting-recording.md).
- [ ] No configuration change reaches users unpublished, and every published version can be rolled back with data kept ([UC-F2-09](../../../02-functional/f02-no-code-configuration/use-cases/05-publish-and-roll-back/uc-f02-09-draft-preview-and-publish-a-change.md), [UC-F2-10](../../../02-functional/f02-no-code-configuration/use-cases/05-publish-and-roll-back/uc-f02-10-roll-a-configuration-back.md), [BR-F2-01](../../../02-functional/f02-no-code-configuration/business-rules/br-f02-01-archive-never-delete.md)).
- [ ] Every screen of P3 is in the company's languages and passes the accessibility checks ([localisation](../../../05-non-functional/localisation.md), [accessibility](../../../05-non-functional/accessibility.md)).
- [ ] The Bruno collection covers every new endpoint of the `admin`, `config`, `ai`, `meetings`, `onboarding`, `billing` and `integrations` services and runs headless on the dev stage (TEST-1).
- [ ] The phase exit suite (T3.95) passes on one commit, with its evidence.

## <a id="dependencies"></a>⛓️ Dependencies

| Needs | From | Why |
| --- | --- | --- |
| The service kit, the company context and rights middleware, the outbox, the metering client, the back-office shell, the design tokens, i18n and the test scaffolds | P0 Foundations | Every P3 service and screen is built on them |
| Companies and data isolation | P2 Platform core, E2.1 (T1) | The setup wizard, client companies, group sharing and closing work on companies |
| Identity and access | P2, E2.2 (T2) | Invitations, roles, record rules, single sign-on, provisioning, sessions and the Configurator role |
| Audit trail | P2, E2.3 (T3) | Configuration versions, signed assistant changes, audit search and SIEM export |
| Plugin runtime | P2, E2.4 (T4) | Record types share the plugins' model, and modules are switched on as plugins |
| API and webhooks | P2, E2.5 (T5) | Custom fields in the API, webhook actions, tokens, meetings to outside tools |
| MCP and A2A gateway | P2, E2.6 (T6) | External tools, the AI assistant's outside calls, meeting MCP tools |
| AI providers and models | P2, E2.7 (T7) | The AI provider per feature, the AI assistant, transcription, workflow AI steps |
| RAG platform | P2, E2.8 (T8) | Answers, knowledge and meeting search within rights |
| Translations and notifications | P2, E2.10 (T10) | Invitations, warnings, labels per language |
| Legal corpora | P2, E2.11 (T11) | SIRENE identity, VAT, legal mentions, the legal check, FEC opening balances |
| Connectors and scrapers | P2, E2.12 (T12) | Calendars, meeting platforms, the catalogue, CRM, ATS and suites |
| Maps and geodata | P2, E2.13 (T13) | The map right of roles and the map view of record types |

P3 prepares P4 onwards: every business capability's records get custom fields, rules, templates, views, the AI assistant and metering from the platform built here.

## <a id="risks"></a>🎲 Risks

| Risk | Why it matters in P3 | Response in P3 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Pages waiting for the owner | Many use cases and rules of P3 (for example the later rounds of F1, F21, F22 and F24) are marked waiting for the owner, and the request types of FL-F2-12 are a draft | Each ticket is built from the page as it stands and its TICKET.md cites the page's commit; a changed page reopens the ticket, not the phase |
| F24 placement is Proposed | The integrations hub may move to a later phase | F24 tickets are kept together in E3.13; outside it only T3.57 (connections in the guide), T3.78 (client video through the suite) and the exit T3.95 need them, and would wait for F24 if it moved |
| The AI assistant shows what it must not | A leak through an answer, an explanation or a shared conversation breaks [BR-F3-02](../../../02-functional/f03-assistant/business-rules/br-f03-02-answers-use-only-what-the-user-may-see.md) and the data isolation requirement | The permission suite runs on every pull request from T3.07; the injection corpus of T3.66; review of the AI assistant tickets first |
| Local model quality | Answers, transcription and drafts depend on the default local model | The evaluation set of T3.66 and the accuracy measures of T3.77 gate every model change; the fallback of [BR-F1-16](../../../02-functional/f01-administration/business-rules/br-f01-16-fall-back-to-the-local-model.md) |
| Scope of 95 tickets in eight sprints | Seven capabilities with 207 use cases | One ticket per flow, flows split only when large; capabilities run side by side; the longest chain, the F2 engine to the exit (T3.06, T3.16, T3.27, T3.37, T3.62, T3.73, T3.84, T3.95), has one ticket per sprint and is reviewed first, so a late ticket on it is seen at once |
| Business capabilities come later | Some P3 use cases write into capabilities built from P4 on: the opening entry and the parallel run of F21 (T3.80, T3.91) need F8 and F13, invoice matching (T3.45) needs F8, actions synced with tasks (T3.87) and HR imports (T3.82) need F9 and F13 | These tickets write through the contracts of `backend/models` and are tested against stand-ins of the receiving service; their end-to-end test is added by the ticket of the receiving capability in its phase |
| Outside APIs change | Suites, CRM, ATS and calendars evolve | Stand-in APIs in the tests, contract checks per connector, the API change detection of T3.47 |
